**1. Brandon Williams — "It's a gift and a curse. I'm almost at his house almost too much." — Brandon Williams on playing with Anthony Melton** [16:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YSuOMkC9cdg&t=965s)

Brandon Williams: "It's great. It's a gift and a curse. I'm almost at his house almost too much. **Unidentified speaker:** "Deni, this is a special world for you. You broke the record for a young Israeli in the NBA. If I told you six years later you'd get to 25 points in the first half and beat **LeBron James**, what would you say to me?"

**Deni Avdija:** "All this reality is a dream for me, and the fans that come to visit me and give me the support and love while I'm far from home, far from family and friends, they have no idea how much it does me good. Tyronn Lue: "It's difficult because you got to now see who plays well with who, you got many restrictions. The person who had to suffer was **Javonte Green**, who's been playing great for us, and so he didn't do anything wrong. I just feel bad for Javonte because he's done everything right, he's played well, and he's going to get another opportunity."

**9. Kevin Durant — "I love that we got more shooting on the floor." — Kevin Durant on the team's improved shooting and efficiency** [01:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tEWB4xc9VeQ&t=105s)

Kevin Durant: "Yeah, I mean I love that we got more shooting on the floor. I think that was the main problem for us last year. The lack of shooting that we had on the floor and the lack of three-point attempts. I'm not saying we going crazy and shooting 40 or 50 threes now, but we are more efficient because we got more shooters out there and that's that simple. I think a lot of that stuff that why people hated last year so much is because we couldn't we didn't knock down a lot of shots from the perimeter. ## Steve Kerr, Warriors analyze bounce-back win after road skid | NBC Sports Bay Area — *Golden State Warriors*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0dCnatHCJbI](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0dCnatHCJbI)

_Speakers identified: Steve Kerr, Al Horford, Gui Santos_

**1. Steve Kerr — "He's automatically going to go to the best perimeter player. When he's at the five, he's often guarding the center." — Draymond Green's defensive versatility at the four and five** [01:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0dCnatHCJbI&t=65s)

Steve Kerr: "He's automatically going to go to the best perimeter player. When he's at the five, he's often guarding the center, so this allows us to put him on somebody like **Jason Tatum**."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0dCnatHCJbI&t=65s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0dCnatHCJbI&t=65s)

**2. Steve Kerr — "We probably have to start teaching our defensive players to flop on screens because everybody around the league is doing it." — Warriors need to teach players to stop flopping** [03:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0dCnatHCJbI&t=180s)

Steve Kerr: "We probably have to start teaching our defensive players to flop on screens because everybody around the league is doing it. You set a screen and guys chase around the screen and act like they've been hit by a car and the whole bench jumps up and those are automatic fouls, so we have to start teaching that."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0dCnatHCJbI&t=180s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0dCnatHCJbI&t=180s)

**3. Steve Kerr — "We've gotten a lot of illegal screens because the ball handler has gone too early in the pick and roll." — Warriors' league-leading illegal screen count is a coaching issue** [04:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0dCnatHCJbI&t=274s)

Steve Kerr: "We've gotten a lot of illegal screens because the ball handler has gone too early in the pick and roll. We've also gotten some where we've just extended our arms out or extended an elbow out or a hip and so we have to be better. We're number one in the league in illegal screens and it's not the officiating, it's us. We have to adapt to how the game is called and to why we're getting these illegal screens."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0dCnatHCJbI&t=274s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0dCnatHCJbI&t=274s)

**4. Steve Kerr — "On the road we got bothered by ball pressure. You can't do that with Brandon, he'll just blow right by you." — Brandon Williams' return will help with ball pressure issues** [08:33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0dCnatHCJbI&t=513s)

Steve Kerr: "On the road we got bothered by ball pressure by a couple of the teams. They took us out in San Antonio, Dallas, Memphis in particular. Those last few games, ball pressure really hurt us, kind of put us on our heels and you can't do that with Brandon, he'll just blow right by you. He'll be a big help and definitely him going out at the same time as Steph was a big blow."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0dCnatHCJbI&t=513s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0dCnatHCJbI&t=513s)

## Jordi Fernández Postgame Press Conference vs. Minnesota Timberwolves — *Brooklyn Nets*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1fDXAjs1Oeg](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1fDXAjs1Oeg)

_Speakers identified: Jordi Fernández_

**1. Jordi Fernández — "I think that we had hands down from the beginning of the game. That's happened already too many times." — Jordi Fernández on defensive lapses and team physicality** [00:11](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1fDXAjs1Oeg&t=11s)

Jordi Fernández: "I think that I cannot answer accurate, but I can say that a lot of things we could have done better, starting with our physicality, ball pressure, contesting shots. I thought that we had hands down from the beginning of the game. That's happened already too many times where we allow teams that you know are the best players in the world, that if you allow one to go in and then everybody gets loose and makes shots, then it's a long night. We allowed 41 to start. We kept fighting, we did good things at times. Obviously we cut it to three, even though at the end right there cut it and keep fighting, but we got to be better. You see our percentage offensively, 53 from the field, 50 from three against the best defense in the NBA, but you give them 21 turnovers for 31 points, so your offense doesn't mean much."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1fDXAjs1Oeg&t=11s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1fDXAjs1Oeg&t=11s)

**2. Jordi Fernández — "Good juice to start. Obviously this physicality is something that he needs to get used to." — Jordi Fernández evaluates Mike Miles Jr.'s debut performance** [01:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1fDXAjs1Oeg&t=76s)

Jordi Fernández: "Good juice to start. Obviously this physicality is something that he needs to get used to and I think those were it's not just about learning, it's about helping the team compete. He did good things I have to watch film, but his intentions were good so I I think that I stay with that and then all the other mistakes, we got to keep pushing him to be better."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1fDXAjs1Oeg&t=76s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1fDXAjs1Oeg&t=76s)

**3. Jordi Fernández — "He is a basketball player and I think that he can playmake and he can also play off the ball." — Jordi Fernández on Yegor Demin's playmaking and versatility** [02:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1fDXAjs1Oeg&t=130s)

Jordi Fernández: "He is a basketball player and I think that he can playmake and he can also play off the ball. I think his superpower is shooting and gravity and I think he does a good job with that and he can help the team to set up and playmake and I think that right there is what I can tell you."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1fDXAjs1Oeg&t=130s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1fDXAjs1Oeg&t=130s)

**4. Jordi Fernández — "I think he can do better with this ball pressure and physicality, that's for sure, communication." — Jordi Fernández on expectations for Yegor Demin's development** [03:09](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1fDXAjs1Oeg&t=189s)

Jordi Fernández: "I think he can do better with this ball pressure and physicality, that's for sure, communication. I think the whole team too contesting shots like I said. And then he's got to keep taking good shots because as we all know, he's a great player and every time he shoots the three, I think it's going in. So I know that right now we're looking at 16 games played, still small sample size, so I'm not going to judge my players for shots going in. I'm going to judge him if the shots are good shots or bad shots. I can judge him obviously in the locker room or inside closed doors if his effort is good, if his physicality is good, it's communication is good and we keep, you know, working and getting better. That's for sure we can do."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1fDXAjs1Oeg&t=189s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1fDXAjs1Oeg&t=189s)

**5. Jordi Fernández — "We need him to be aggressive to get his spots, to run, to move, to be hard to guard." — Jordi Fernández on Mike Miles Jr.'s need for aggression** [04:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1fDXAjs1Oeg&t=255s)

Jordi Fernández: "Like you said, it comes with like the purpose and that being aggressive. I think Mike does a great job taking care of his body and how strong he is and we need him to be aggressive to get his spots, to run, to move, to be hard to guard, to cut to the rim and I think he can do that. Tonight was not the night and then I expect him to be great in Milwaukee. So again, he faced pretty good defense, but I still think that he could have been better and I know he will."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1fDXAjs1Oeg&t=255s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1fDXAjs1Oeg&t=255s)

## Doug Christie, Keegan Murray, De’Andre Hunter recap Kings’ loss | NBC Sports California — *Sacramento Kings*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0)

_Speakers identified: Keegan Murray, De’Andre Hunter, Doug Christie_

**1. Keegan Murray — "they were just able to get second chance points and second quarter really hurt us" — Keegan Murray on the team's defensive struggles against second-chance points** [00:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=14s)

Keegan Murray: "I mean it started in the second quarter with their offensive rebounds and at that point they were just able to get second chance points and second quarter really hurt us and at some point in the third quarter hurt us as well and that's when the game got away from us."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=14s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=14s)

**2. Keegan Murray — "you have to be physical with him at the point of the screen" — Keegan Murray on the defensive challenges of guarding Duncan Robinson** [00:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=46s)

Keegan Murray: "**Duncan Robinson** is just a guy that runs off screens. You have to be physical with him at the point of the screen because if he has any daylight he is just going to put it up and he is a really good shooter. Our backside help wasn't great. They hit him a lot when the corner net rolled and he was on the weak side and they hit that skip pass a lot and he was open."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=46s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=46s)

**3. Keegan Murray — "it's the guards that have to come in, rebound and help" — Keegan Murray on the team's defensive rebounding responsibilities** [01:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=98s)

Keegan Murray: "It's a lot on threes. Boston is a team that shoots a lot of threes which means long rebounds and at that point it's the guards that have to come in, rebound and help. It's not on **Domantas Sabonis**, it's not on **Precious Achiuwa**, it's not on **Dylan Windler**, none of those guys because they have to handle the big guy down low and we're getting beat with their guards crashing on offensive rebounds. Our guards just got to get better, we got to be more physical, boxing people out."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=98s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=98s)

**4. Keegan Murray — "our record shows we're not a good team because we have lapses in the middle of games" — Keegan Murray on the team's lack of consistency and lapses** [02:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=143s)

Keegan Murray: "Right now our record shows we're not a good team because we have lapses in the middle of games like that. Teams like San Antonio, even with guys out, they're relentless. The guys come off the bench, know what they have to do and they didn't have any lapses. So for us to be at that level, regardless of who plays, you just got to figure it out and there's no excuse for that."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=143s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=143s)

**5. De’Andre Hunter — "I think we need more player movement. I think we kind of stalled out" — De’Andre Hunter on the team's offensive stagnation against the zone** [03:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=182s)

De’Andre Hunter: "I think we need more player movement. I think we kind of stalled out, just passed it around the perimeter and you know they kind of forced us to take contested threes."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=182s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=182s)

**6. De’Andre Hunter — "I think Duncan Robinson getting hot, I think that was the second quarter" — De’Andre Hunter on Duncan Robinson's impact on the game** [03:25](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=205s)

De’Andre Hunter: "I think **Duncan Robinson** getting hot, I think that was the second quarter, you know he brought them back in the game and I think that gave them life and I think they just carried that throughout the game. They started making shots, they started feeling comfortable and you know we didn't do a good job making them uncomfortable."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=205s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=205s)

**7. De’Andre Hunter — "I think their zone slowed us down a lot. We couldn't get stops" — De’Andre Hunter on the impact of the opponent's zone defense** [04:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=243s)

De’Andre Hunter: "I think their zone slowed us down a lot. We couldn't get stops, which stopped us from running in transition, which I think that's when we're at our best. It forced us to play half-court basketball and I think we were just sloppy offensively."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=243s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=243s)

**8. De’Andre Hunter — "it's just attention to detail and effort. I think that's the biggest thing" — De’Andre Hunter on the importance of defensive rebounding and effort** [05:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=334s)

De’Andre Hunter: "It's just attention to detail and effort. I think that's the biggest thing. Obviously we're a little undersized with **Domantas Sabonis** at the five, but I think we still can, you know, defensive rebound a lot better and stop the other team from getting offensive rebounds. So I think that's where we lose a lot of our games at and you know we just have to concentrate and focus on that and probably not worry about other things as much."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=334s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6a0LEuClNm0&t=334s)

## Doug Christie addresses Kings' 'low' energy after loss to Spurs | NBC Sports California — *Sacramento Kings*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=82DPXULLSNE](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=82DPXULLSNE)

_Speakers identified: Doug Christie_

**1. Doug Christie — "I thought the energy just was low. I talked to them about discipline and consistency." — Doug Christie on the Kings' low energy and lack of discipline** [00:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=82DPXULLSNE&t=22s)

Doug Christie: "I thought the energy just was low. You know, bottling up that energy that we had to get to that is I talked to them about discipline and consistency. And you know, a hot guy, you can't go under. You go under, he hits a shot, he sees it go in and all of a sudden, he's in fuego. You got to make sure that you step up, that you turn his water off immediately. But, you know, a disciplined team, they're going to continue on running their stuff."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=82DPXULLSNE&t=22s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=82DPXULLSNE&t=22s)

**2. Doug Christie — "It has to be a gang rebound mentality. Everyone has to come back and help out." — Doug Christie emphasizes the need for a gang rebounding mentality** [01:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=82DPXULLSNE&t=72s)

Doug Christie: "The offensive rebounds, this is the second game that we've given up 20 offensive rebounds. And it has to be a gang rebound mentality. Everyone has to come back and help out on the backboard. And that doesn't mean turning and looking, and I'm sure when I go back and watch this, I'm going to see a lot of that. And it's you got to check your guy first, hit him, and then pursue the basketball."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=82DPXULLSNE&t=72s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=82DPXULLSNE&t=72s)

**3. Doug Christie — "When the shot goes up, you have to check your man. That's what you have to do." — Doug Christie explains the importance of checking your man on rebounds** [02:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=82DPXULLSNE&t=128s)

Doug Christie: "It's a focus thing because when the shot goes up, you have to check your man. That's what you have to do. And I'm pretty sure when I go back, I'm going to see a lot of guys just looking and then they're just running right at the rim. That is just attention to detail. Is there a little energy in that? Yeah, there is, because you got to stay locked in and make sure that you're checking your guy off."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=82DPXULLSNE&t=128s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=82DPXULLSNE&t=128s)

**4. Doug Christie — "He was a little under the weather. But he's a competitor, I'm not worried about him." — Doug Christie addresses De'Aaron Fox's performance and health status** [03:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=82DPXULLSNE&t=182s)

Doug Christie: "He was a little under the weather. But you know, I mean, he probably wouldn't say that, but he was. Just a fact and tried to work him through it. I thought his energy got better as the game went on. But yeah, I mean, first time back-to-back, first time three in four nights, but he's a competitor, I'm not worried about him. But you know, the health part of it, sometimes you're feeling it, sometimes you're not."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=82DPXULLSNE&t=182s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=82DPXULLSNE&t=182s)

## Mitch Johnson on Different Spurs Stepping Up vs. Sacramento — *San Antonio Spurs*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FyZDPUfTViY](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FyZDPUfTViY)

_Speakers identified: Mitch Johnson_

**1. Mitch Johnson — "we played a good amount of minutes last year without Victor with a lot of the same cast" — Mitch Johnson on the value of non-Wembanyama minutes** [00:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FyZDPUfTViY&t=2s)

**Unidentified reporter:** "Obviously a lot has been made about the non-Wembanyama minutes this year. Did you view this as a good chance to see 48 of them and how different combinations could gel that way?"

**Mitch Johnson:** "Yeah, and again, we played a good amount of minutes last year without Victor with a lot of the same cast. And typically, time helps chemistry, execution, feel. And that group has had limited time a lot this year because Victor has played well and he's played minutes. And so it's just a very fine program win in terms of a lot of work that went into tonight that you don't necessarily see because you don't necessarily know when you'll need to tap into some of the things that we did tonight."

As I've said, I've had to go off script at times and pull another guy, and even if he's in there just for three minutes, five minutes, then he finds himself in the rotation a game or two later. We've been able to benefit from that because it allows me to get more information about each individual player, how they're fitting into our system, how they're fitting into our culture on the court. Being able to have a healthy roster allows us to have that. Obviously, we have to make difficult decisions when we got a full 18-man healthy roster, but hopefully in the long run, it allows us to have the development, have the data on each individual guy."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GSeb2kM6nAM&t=145s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GSeb2kM6nAM&t=145s)

Taylor Jenkins: "We have a belief in how we operate. Obviously, this is our first year together and you're hopeful that in the off-season, you only have so much control in the off-season, working with guys and how they're building their bodies up for the marathon run. We've been very intentional even with limited practice time, we're going maybe against the normal a little bit where we're actually putting in higher intense work day after a game and adjusting what our game day, the next day looks like so we have our energy. Hopefully that is, we're still gathering the data, but I do think there's something to the work. You want to build up robust, resilient players."

## Taylor Jenkins Postgame Press Conference | 11.23.26 — *Milwaukee Bucks*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HPcUSVAXQS8](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HPcUSVAXQS8)

Taylor Jenkins: "It was great to see our guys put 48 minutes together. I mean, you think you look at the start of the first quarter and the start of the third quarter, we didn't get off to the right starts both quarters there, but our guys just were resilient all night. Obviously, we had a tough one against them in our first game back in Milwaukee, and this was going to be a tough one again. The physicality, obviously, Barnes, heck of a player. I thought we did a great job throughout the game just trying to make him take tough shots, but he's hard to shut down. I thought our rotations, for the most part, were really good, and then just trying to keep them off the boards. I mean, they had 31 second-chance points in the first game. We talked about it at halftime, 14 for the game, that's a big deciding factor."

## Kenrich Williams on Team's Mindset | Postgame Media | November 23, 2026 — *Oklahoma City Thunder*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LGw8NJ2bfNE](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LGw8NJ2bfNE)

_Speakers identified: Kenrich Williams_

**1. Kenrich Williams — "It's easy to make excuses in this league, but as long as you compete, you give yourself a chance." — Kenrich Williams on the importance of competing in every game** [00:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LGw8NJ2bfNE&t=14s)

Kenrich Williams: "I think we came out and competed. That's the main battle, is competing, not laying down. ## Ayo Dosunmu Postgame Interview With Alan Horton | 11.23.26 — *Minnesota Timberwolves*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=MG6wmc5S1ec](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=MG6wmc5S1ec)

_Speakers identified: Alan Horton, Ayo Dosunmu_

**1. Ayo Dosunmu — "We have a bunch of different weapons, a bunch of different guys that can make plays." — Ayo Dosunmu on the Timberwolves' offensive aggression and team depth** [00:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=MG6wmc5S1ec&t=16s)

**Unidentified reporter:** "The defensive effort wasn't quite there, but it didn't need to be tonight because your offense was really flowing. What was the key to the offense tonight and guys just knocking down shots all over?"

**Ayo Dosunmu:** "Just being aggressive. I think that's the main thing. We have a bunch of different weapons, a bunch of different guys that can make plays, so we were able to do that. **Jaden McDaniels** was able to get to his spots. He led us tonight in scoring, and it was fascinating to watch."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=MG6wmc5S1ec&t=16s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=MG6wmc5S1ec&t=16s)

**Alan Horton:** "A career high for **Jaden McDaniels**, 34 points, 10 rebounds, 6 assists, 12 of 16 shooting. Is this a glimpse of what Jaden is? He's just gotten better and better every year he's been in the league. You joined him last season, but the world's at his fingertips, right?"

**Ayo Dosunmu:** "For sure. He's playing aggressive, he's playing efficient. He's seeing the ball go in, he knows he's getting his shots set throughout the offense, and he's an efficient basketball player. Micah Nori: "**Danny** carried us obviously offensively in the first half, and then in the second half **Scoot Henderson** came in the basketball game and got hot and was able to generate some buckets for us when we needed them. **Drew Holiday** hit some big ones and to your point with 32 assists and 46 percent on 19 made threes, it was a collective effort."

Micah Nori: "Quite honestly, we did overhelp a little bit too much and they did miss some open looks. The one thing that we talked about doing was making sure that it wasn't easy. ## Lakers vs Jazz | Postgame Interviews x Highlights: November 23, 2026 — *Los Angeles Lakers*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8)

_Speakers identified: JJ Redick, Cameron Carr, Walker Kessler, Jake Laravia_

**1. JJ Redick — "We need to be better with our closeout discipline and even against lasers we got to be better." — JJ Redick on the need for better closeout discipline** [00:29](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=29s)

JJ Redick: "We need to be better with our closeout discipline and even against lasers we got to be better. When you are whether you are in a shift or you are in rotation and you have a laser and you literally run at him and you jump from 10 feet away and you fly by him, that is actually doing nothing because he is just going to take a side step three and he is going to get the same thing that we were just trying to give up."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=29s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=29s)

**2. JJ Redick — "They are all really grateful to be here, to be Lakers, they are all really grateful to be on this team." — JJ Redick on the team's collective gratitude and selflessness** [03:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=186s)

JJ Redick: "They are all really grateful to be here, to be Lakers, they are all really grateful to be on this team. I know **Matis** has expressed that to me on a couple of occasions. They have experienced adversity in their careers in different ways and I think that gratitude piece is they are all in a situation where they are putting the team first and they are putting their egos aside and that stuff matters."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=186s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=186s)

**3. JJ Redick — "When you are on a good team, you have to value the G League and what that is." — JJ Redick on the importance of G League development** [05:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=306s)

JJ Redick: "When you are on a good team, you have to value the G League and what that is. It was literally started, it is called the development league. It is for development and when you are on a good team, you have to get reps as a young player. I think **Zack** has done a great job over the last two years, **Alex** is doing a fantastic job this year."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=306s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=306s)

**4. Cameron Carr — "Overall we did a really good job. Of course there is still things we can get better at." — Cameron Carr on defensive consistency and the fourth quarter group** [07:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=432s)

**Cameron Carr:** "Honestly just defensive lapses as I said earlier today, just trying to be consistent and fill gaps and be where I need to be on time and don't be laxadaisical on defense."

**Unidentified reporter:** "And what did you make of how that went tonight and then really the the whole defensive effort tonight?"

**Cameron Carr:** "Overall we did a really good job. Of course there is still things we can get better at. I think the fourth quarter group that started, that was that was that group kind of put us over the top right there."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=432s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=432s)

**5. Walker Kessler — "Our transition defense has just gotten better, which is nice to see." — Walker Kessler on the team's improved transition defense** [12:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=737s)

Walker Kessler: "Our transition defense has just gotten better, which is nice to see. Limiting second chance points has gotten better. Obviously we can still improve on that. We are kind of struggling right now with open threes, but I feel like we had a tough stretch where they kind of came back in the second half."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=737s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=737s)

**6. Walker Kessler — "Our defensive coach Greg St. Jean and I have kind of talked about the zone." — Walker Kessler on the team's defensive zone adjustments** [13:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=803s)

Walker Kessler: "Our defensive coach **Greg St. Jean** and I have kind of talked about the zone and we have switched it up because in the beginning of the year we were kind of icing on the sides and giving up down, but now we are seeing people in the middle. So we have been workshopping it, but it was good to see it worked for tonight."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=803s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=803s)

**7. Walker Kessler — "Seeing how we compete without him and not at our full strength is good, it builds resiliency." — Walker Kessler on team resiliency without Austin Reaves** [16:26](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=986s)

Walker Kessler: "Seeing how we compete without him and not at our full strength is good, it builds resiliency because there is not going to be always your full strength going into the season, things happen to a long season. So it is encouraging to see, but obviously we miss him and excited for him to get back out there."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=986s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=986s)

**8. Jake Laravia — "Everyone is willing to sacrifice for the same goal and we just have a lot of adaptable players." — Jake Laravia on the team's shared goals and adaptability** [20:53](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=1253s)

Jake Laravia: "Everyone is willing to sacrifice for the same goal and we just have a lot of adaptable players. A lot of it is lineup based, matchup based, so whoever the coaches think should start that night is who is going to start and I think as players we are all willing to be thrown in and out of the lineup and it doesn't really change much for us as players, you just keep the same mindset and keep the same goal and just go out there and play hard."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=1253s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=1253s)

**9. Jake Laravia — "I think our half-court defense is probably the best part of our defense." — Jake Laravia on the team's half-court defensive strength** [23:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=1402s)

Jake Laravia: "I think our half-court defense is probably the best part of our defense. We break it up kind of eight seconds, that middle eight seconds of being in the half-court is our best defense and we do a really good job of just moving around and scrambling and being in each other's help and trusting each other to do their job."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=1402s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjILqPXKQC8&t=1402s)

So, just playing off of different players and understanding your role when certain guys are back. So it's not just it's nothing different, it's just understanding how to play with certain guys, like playing the game with Scotty and playing the game with **Quickley** and just having those guys out there and knowing your role with those guys."

## Jalen Green Postgame On-Court Interview after Suns win against New Orleans Pelicans 123-119 — *Phoenix Suns*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UQChnqoeqn8](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UQChnqoeqn8)

_Speakers identified: Amanda, Jalen Green_

**1. Jalen Green — "We just had to stay locked in defensively. That's where a lot of our lapses was." — Jalen Green on the Suns' defensive focus in overtime win** [00:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UQChnqoeqn8&t=4s)

**Unidentified reporter:** "Jalen, you look, this Pelicans team wouldn't go away. They had a season-high 67 bench points. How were you guys able to lock in and get this one done in overtime?"

**Jalen Green:** "We just had to stay locked in defensively. That's where a lot of our lapses was. It's tough, it's a road game, they're at home. A lot of teams play better when they're at home, and they came out and put out a fight. You can't come out here and just relax. You got to come out and play hard, so that's what we did."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UQChnqoeqn8&t=4s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UQChnqoeqn8&t=4s)

<small>[Clip it](presserclips://clip?v=UQChnqoeqn8&t=4&q=1) · [Send to Slack](presserclips://clip?v=UQChnqoeqn8&t=4&q=1&slack=1)</small>

**2. Jalen Green — "Communication is the biggest key for us defensively. When we're talking and flying around, that's when we're at our best." — Jalen Green on maintaining energy and defensive communication** [00:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UQChnqoeqn8&t=46s)

**Unidentified reporter:** "You were locked in, a season-high 25 points for you tonight. I want to talk about just the late-game execution from you. How are you able to continue to bring that type of energy each time? Talking to **Miles Bridges** today at shootaround, and he said the biggest factor in these wins for you guys is really your defense. What have you found there?"

**Jalen Green:** "Just staying aggressive, continuing to be me, trust my work, simple. Communication is the biggest key for us defensively. When we're talking and flying around, that's when we're at our best, getting steals, and then our offense just flows matching me. So, just got to continue to double down on defense, locking on film, and work towards it."

**2. Steve Kerr — "His speed is a real weapon for us, and it'll be great to have him back." — Steve Kerr on Brandon Williams' speed and impact on defense** [00:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YSuOMkC9cdg&t=45s)

I'm going to take the ball right past you and create something.' His speed is a real weapon for us, and it'll be great to have him back."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YSuOMkC9cdg&t=45s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YSuOMkC9cdg&t=45s)

**3. Steve Kerr — "There's just a different vibe when Steph is in the building." — Steve Kerr on the emotional impact of Stephen Curry's presence** [01:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YSuOMkC9cdg&t=75s)

Steve Kerr: "He's always a breath of fresh air. The humor and the confidence that he has. You saw the other night, Draymond hits a three from the corner and he's flying all over the place on the bench. There's just a different vibe when Steph is in the building, and it's good to have him here even if he's not playing."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YSuOMkC9cdg&t=75s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YSuOMkC9cdg&t=75s)

**4. Steve Kerr — "I love the internal leadership we have on this team. Our vets have been fantastic." — Steve Kerr on the value of veteran leadership on the team** [01:48](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YSuOMkC9cdg&t=108s)

Steve Kerr: "I love the internal leadership we have on this team. Our vets have been fantastic. Draymond's leadership every day has been brilliant. Al and Steph are more the quiet leaders by example, but pull a guy aside and talk to him. Our veteran players have provided such great leadership for us this year, and it's really helpful, especially for the young guys and Brandon in particular, given how much he's playing."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YSuOMkC9cdg&t=108s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YSuOMkC9cdg&t=108s)

**5. Steve Kerr — "You can't learn it all in 15 games. There's a big learning curve." — Steve Kerr on managing the learning curve for young players** [02:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YSuOMkC9cdg&t=151s)

Steve Kerr: "We're trying to simplify the message. Understand that there's a lot to learn, and you can't learn it all in 15 games. He's going to make mistakes, so it's fine. Just try to learn and go, and understand that it's going to take time for anybody. Even the best players in the world, you look at Wembanyama's first year, he had some great games, but he was nowhere near what he is now. Same with LeBron or Steph Curry's rookie year compared to now. There's a big learning curve, but it's really about letting him play and teaching him without hammering home too many things."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YSuOMkC9cdg&t=151s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YSuOMkC9cdg&t=151s)

Steve Kerr: "Six or seven years ago, **James Wiseman** and I went off of Twitter together, and I realized almost right away how blissful it was. I have not been back, and so a lot of this stuff happens and I'm unaware of it, which is probably a good thing for my mental health given the position I'm in and all the critiquing that comes our way."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YSuOMkC9cdg&t=261s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YSuOMkC9cdg&t=261s)

**7. Steve Kerr — "It's never been more difficult to be an athlete than it is today." — Steve Kerr on the difficulty of being an athlete today** [05:25](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YSuOMkC9cdg&t=325s)

Steve Kerr: "It's never been more difficult to be an athlete than it is today because of social media, because of the eyes that are on every single one of their moves on and off the court. It's very, very difficult what these guys face every day."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YSuOMkC9cdg&t=325s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YSuOMkC9cdg&t=325s)

Steve Kerr: "One of the things I've talked to him about is the realities of this job. When you get into the NBA, you should expect boos, injuries, trades, contract disputes. Some people do it like I do and ignore it. Everyone has to figure out for themselves."

## Al Horford details what sparked Warriors’ turnaround vs. Celtics | NBC Sports BA — *Golden State Warriors*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A)

_Speakers identified: Al Horford_

**1. Al Horford — "the urgency was there, you know, I think that's where it starts, losing five in a row" — Al Horford on the team's defensive urgency and recent struggles** [00:09](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=9s)

Al Horford: "I think the urgency was there, you know, I think that's where it starts, losing five in a row, just very difficult and being back home and just being able to refocus and I think it started with that urgency, how hard we were playing on the defensive end and how locked in we were into the game plan, that's what gave us the best opportunity and now we have to make sure that we validated and come out on Wednesday and keep that same type of intensity."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=9s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=9s)

**2. Al Horford — "we were doing good but it wasn't good enough, the effort level" — Al Horford on the team's film session and defensive effort** [00:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=59s)

Al Horford: "Usually something that we do, addressing the different things, but I think the coaches made it very clear that we needed to be better and the biggest thing, it's not so much the coverages and the schemes, it was we were doing good but it wasn't good enough, the effort level and I think that we were challenged in that way and to be able to really mentally push through different things that we're all going through, so I think that was very positive."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=59s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=59s)

**3. Al Horford — "it's great to have him just because his ability to put pressure on the rim" — Al Horford on Brandon Williams' return and impact on offense** [01:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=117s)

Al Horford: "It's great to have him just because his ability to put pressure on the rim and just the energy that **Brandon Williams** brings, so I'm just excited for him to be back with our group and just to get reacclimated with us."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=117s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=117s)

**4. Al Horford — "we have a lot of people that are in his ear, a lot of people" — Al Horford on the team's support for Jaxson Hayes** [02:24](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=144s)

Al Horford: "I haven't been able to really to speak to him much. I feel like we have a lot of people that are in his ear, a lot of people, a lot of advice from all different areas coming to him and I feel like we all mean well a lot of the time but sometimes it can be a little much and we want what's best for him, we want him to come along quickly and everything, but I just think that sometimes I just kind of observe and I see and wherever I can help him I will, but he's a very smart player, smart individual and it's a lot of information, it's a lot of things that go on and it's a long season."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=144s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=144s)

**5. Al Horford — "the understanding that we don't have the luxury of what that best player brings" — Al Horford on the challenge of playing without Stephen Curry** [03:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=212s)

Al Horford: "I think it's the understanding that we don't have the luxury of what that best player brings and with Steph, so much attention, everything that he draws just opens up things for us and I think that now we have to work much harder to create those advantages and to be able to whether it's generate pressure on the rim or taking the open shots or taking advantage of those opportunities, so I think that consistently we have to find ways to be very efficient and there can be a lot of room for mistakes, turnovers, things like that."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=212s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=212s)

**6. Al Horford — "the biggest thing for Bassey, it has to do with his confidence" — Al Horford on the growth and confidence of Charles Bassey** [06:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=374s)

Al Horford: "I think that the biggest thing for Bassey, it has to do with his confidence. I think that he, as he's getting more comfortable here and understanding what we expect and need from him, it just seems that he continues to grow in his role. He's getting more confident on the offensive end on when he needs to finish what he needs to do and then defensively he's starting to anchor that defense and block shots and be a presence and be there, so I just think it's the time and then he's a much more confident player than when we got him at the end of last season."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=374s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=374s)

**7. Al Horford — "it's a two-man game when it comes to a screen" — Al Horford on the team's discipline with setting screens** [07:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=422s)

Al Horford: "It's always a fine line with that stuff. For us, it's making sure that we just have to be disciplined and also making sure that it's like a two-man game when it comes to a screen, so the person that's receiving the screen, he has to be patient and then the one that's setting the screen, we want to make sure that we get ourselves the right angle but also that we're not necessarily moving and things like that, so it works with both things, it's an emphasis the league has been doing and we just have to adjust to it and make sure that we set our man up and then that we also are able to set good legal screens."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=422s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Yk7vJLrjZ1A&t=422s)

## Coach Daigneault on OKC's Compete Level | Postgame Media | November 23, 2026 — *Oklahoma City Thunder*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dCK9SdzM-ME](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dCK9SdzM-ME)

_Speakers identified: Mark Daigneault_

**1. Mark Daigneault — "defensively we left something to be desired tonight. Obviously our margins are a little thin with our size" — Mark Daigneault on OKC's defensive shortcomings and thin margins** [00:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dCK9SdzM-ME&t=10s)

Mark Daigneault: "I thought obviously offensively we were pretty good tonight. 120 points is enough to win anywhere, home or road. Defensively we left something to be desired tonight. Obviously our margins are a little thin with our size, but that's a gift. If you want to get better, it really puts the spotlight on the need to be really tight on multiple possessions and they put a lot of pressure on you offensively obviously with the pace and the pace in the halfcourt. So we were behind a lot of plays where we could have been better, but I thought we really competed."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dCK9SdzM-ME&t=10s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dCK9SdzM-ME&t=10s)

<small>[Clip it](presserclips://clip?v=dCK9SdzM-ME&t=10&q=1) · [Send to Slack](presserclips://clip?v=dCK9SdzM-ME&t=10&q=1&slack=1)</small>

**2. Mark Daigneault — "we're playing small and the margins on that are thinner, especially when you're going against size" — Mark Daigneault on the challenges of playing small ball** [01:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dCK9SdzM-ME&t=70s)

Mark Daigneault: "We're playing small and the margins on that are thinner, especially when you're going against size. I thought that showed up last night and tonight. So some of it is structural despite the effort, but it just puts that much more of an emphasis on the need to execute. And like I said, it's a gift. These games are tough. We're giving ourselves a chance in some of these closer games where a couple plays away and that puts the spotlight on every single detail, every single possession and that's an opportunity for us to look in the mirror and improve because obviously it stings when you lose."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dCK9SdzM-ME&t=70s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dCK9SdzM-ME&t=70s)

Mark Daigneault: "He does it on both ends, does it on the glass, good offensive rebounder, good defensive rebounder. ## Jaden McDaniels Postgame Interview After Win Against Brooklyn Nets | 11.23.26 — *Minnesota Timberwolves*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=fI6kwPgbuNo](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=fI6kwPgbuNo)

_Speakers identified: Unidentified speaker, Jaden McDaniels_

## Gui Santos explains Warriors’ trust in rookie Yaxel Lendeborg | NBC Sports BA — *Golden State Warriors*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m3cwC19-AoA](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m3cwC19-AoA)

_Speakers identified: Gui Santos_

**1. Gui Santos — "my body is going in a better direction right now. I'm feeling slowly I'm building up" — Gui Santos on his physical recovery and team chemistry** [00:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m3cwC19-AoA&t=17s)

Gui Santos: "It feels good, especially after a win. The way that we played yesterday, the whole team was playing connected. We were talking a lot about that on, all right, if you're not going to score 120 points, so if we're going to score 88 points, we got to make them score 87 or less. But talking about me, my body is going in a better direction right now. I'm feeling slowly I'm building up and going back to how it was last year, body-wise, and that's a big part of my confidence going up again, a big part of what I do to help the team, being offensive rebounds, be fighting for every possession, and I think going forward now, I will still take care of my body and bring that to the team every night."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m3cwC19-AoA&t=17s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m3cwC19-AoA&t=17s)

**2. Gui Santos — "when I go there and I grab a offense rebound early in the game, that give me" — Gui Santos on the energy boost from offensive rebounds** [01:33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m3cwC19-AoA&t=93s)

Gui Santos: "When I go there and I grab a offense rebound early in the game, that give me a lot of energy because you see, all right, I'm bringing an extra possession for the team. And when you hit a three after that, so like last game, I get in the first possession and I hit a three, then I got two offensive rebounds after that and that's put you in the game, that put you in a better position to feel good about yourself in the game, and the rest will come if you play hard."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m3cwC19-AoA&t=93s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m3cwC19-AoA&t=93s)

**3. Gui Santos — "we winning the game, we cannot give up a two right now. So whatever it takes" — Gui Santos on his defensive mindset during crunch time** [02:24](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m3cwC19-AoA&t=144s)

Gui Santos: "I was not expecting them to call foul on that play, but I was like, okay, we're winning, we're winning the game, we cannot give up a two right now. So whatever it takes to put the body in the middle of the way to him to not score that one. Even let's say if they call the foul before, even if I wasn't there, that could be an and-one and that put them in an even better possession. So my thoughts was just like, he cannot score the easy two points after the foul. So just be there, be present, and trying to make him miss."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m3cwC19-AoA&t=144s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m3cwC19-AoA&t=144s)

**4. Gui Santos — "when you play with Steph so much easier, everything. The space, you always open" — Gui Santos on the challenge of playing without Stephen Curry** [03:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m3cwC19-AoA&t=197s)

Gui Santos: "When you play with Steph so much easier, everything. The space, you always open, he always got two guys guarding him and that's so easy. And when you don't have him, it's just kind of weird because you got to create your own stuff. Everybody got to create their own shots or create for everybody else. When we don't have him out there, it's just a change of mindset because just because he's in a corner, we're playing four-on-four, because they're not going to leave him. And when he's not on the floor, the guy that is in the corner now is in the help. So even if you drive by somebody, there is somebody helping and we start moving the ball."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m3cwC19-AoA&t=197s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m3cwC19-AoA&t=197s)

**5. Gui Santos — "Yaxel has been phenomenal this year for us, you know. He's been playing really well" — Gui Santos on Yaxel Lendeborg's role and team trust** [04:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m3cwC19-AoA&t=282s)

Gui Santos: "Yaxel has been phenomenal this year for us, you know. He's been playing really well and the team trusts him, the staff trusts him, and we know that he's going to do the right decision when he got the ball. So whenever we can, we can give the ball to him in the middle of the paint or either playing pick-and-roll, he's going to have the best decision. But sometimes I feel like the teams are helping and over-helping on him and that's put us in a situation to have the ball doing kind of like the Jimmy role, and he's been doing amazing, but we also got to be there to help him and make his life easier."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m3cwC19-AoA&t=282s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m3cwC19-AoA&t=282s)

Every night I come out here and I play against a guy like him, I try to step up to the challenge as best as I can. ## Houston Rockets Postgame: Kevin Durant talks big fourth quarter and win — *Houston Rockets*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tEWB4xc9VeQ](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tEWB4xc9VeQ)

_Speakers identified: Kevin Durant_

**1. Kevin Durant — "They looking at our stats and seeing guys stepping up knocking down shots." — Kevin Durant on why the Clippers didn't double-team him** [00:29](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tEWB4xc9VeQ&t=29s)

Kevin Durant: "No, I mean you look at our team. That was our 18th game of the year. They looking at our stats and seeing guys stepping up knocking down shots. You see other teams give up easy shots to try to stop guys in the paint or stop me on a pick and roll. So I felt like they just tried to play solid defense tonight and they did a good job of switching up coverages throughout the possession which threw us off a bit. But I think we got our rhythm."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tEWB4xc9VeQ&t=29s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tEWB4xc9VeQ&t=29s)

**2. Kevin Durant — "I feel like we had to make multiple passes tonight." — Kevin Durant on the team's offensive flow and ball movement** [01:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tEWB4xc9VeQ&t=65s)

Kevin Durant: "No, I just think it was a flowing game. I don't think coach we haven't talked about it at all. I know that's been a topic of discussion a lot throughout the season thus far but I feel like we had to make multiple passes tonight. They did a good job on our initial actions and this season our initial actions been getting us some good looks. So sometimes the ball is not going to find guys on that backside when we just pick and roll dunk or pick and roll lob or catch and shoot three. So tonight they made us work a little bit harder and the ball found me a little bit more. Outside of **Darius Garland** going crazy in the start of the third quarter, I feel like we had the game under control."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tEWB4xc9VeQ&t=65s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tEWB4xc9VeQ&t=65s)

**3. Kevin Durant — "I love that we got more shooting on the floor." — Kevin Durant on the team's improved shooting and efficiency** [01:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tEWB4xc9VeQ&t=105s)

Kevin Durant: "Yeah, I mean I love that we got more shooting on the floor. I think that was the main problem for us last year. The lack of shooting that we had on the floor and the lack of three-point attempts. I'm not saying we going crazy and shooting 40 or 50 threes now, but we are more efficient because we got more shooters out there and that's that simple. I think a lot of that stuff that why people hated last year so much is because we couldn't we didn't knock down a lot of shots from the perimeter. So I feel like we got guys that are shooting with confidence and we just getting them up too."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tEWB4xc9VeQ&t=105s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tEWB4xc9VeQ&t=105s)

**4. Kevin Durant — "I just think that we know that we had that game, we could have won easily." — Kevin Durant on bouncing back defensively after a loss** [02:33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tEWB4xc9VeQ&t=153s)

Kevin Durant: "I just think overall we just don't like losing. I just think that we know that we had that game, we could have won easily. So yeah, I mean each individual knows exactly what happened on the defensive side of the ball and you dig down deep, hold yourself accountable internally and come back just with a better effort. It's a long season, you know. We playing against last game we played against a championship-level organization. Offense is a part of our league now, so it's going to be times where we can't get a stop. It's that simple. They hit tough shots on us, but that's the name of the game sometimes. So we can't get discouraged, we got to come back to the next game and play with that same energy and effort and tonight we was able to put our pressure on them a little bit, put our hands on them a little bit and get to cruise out to a good W."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tEWB4xc9VeQ&t=153s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tEWB4xc9VeQ&t=153s)

## Houston Rockets Postgame: Ime Udoka talks win over Clippers — *Houston Rockets*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4)

_Speakers identified: Ime Udoka_

**1. Ime Udoka — "when they did switch, I think him and Alperen had a good chemistry on that two-man game" — Ime Udoka on Kevin Porter Jr.'s chemistry with Alperen Sengun** [00:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4&t=6s)

Ime Udoka: "I wouldn't say specifically that. He was going well in the first half, and when they did switch, I think him and Alperen had a good chemistry on that two-man game. They're going to have their advantage and the doubles came and they moved it. Trying to get him the ball all the time and so some nights they double, some nights they let him go one-on-one and he exploits it. So, taking what the game gives him and like the matchups he had tonight."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4&t=6s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4&t=6s)

**2. Ime Udoka — "for the most part kept those guys in check, got them off the three-point line" — Ime Udoka on defensive discipline against the Clippers** [00:56](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4&t=56s)

Ime Udoka: "Attention to detail was great. They are a heavy three-point shooting team. To hold them to 28 points, I think 8%, only 11 makes, seems a little bit loud when Garland hits three in a row, but for the most part kept those guys in check, got them off the three-point line, which they heavily rely on. So I like our attention to detail on certain personnel, stay down on some Ingram pump fakes, and give them some fouls and buckets there, but overall 104 is 104."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4&t=56s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4&t=56s)

**3. Ime Udoka — "collectively I feel like we have good balance and a variety of ways to get it done" — Ime Udoka on offensive balance and team versatility** [01:36](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4&t=96s)

Ime Udoka: "It's been solid overall. We're improved offensively and everybody has individually, but collectively I feel like we have good balance and a variety of ways to get it done. We want to obviously play with pace and cause some turnovers to get out, and I think we're really good when we do that. But also half-court execution is a big part of it. I love the fact that we got our ISO guys, we got one of the best post players in the league, as well as Kevin on the block, and several different pick-and-roll handlers, several different shooters, so it's not relying on one style of play. We can go double big, we can go small or regular, and the variety is good."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4&t=96s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4&t=96s)

**4. Ime Udoka — "his energy and the way he kind of reeks havoc off the bench is great for us" — Ime Udoka on Tari Eason's impact off the bench** [02:28](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4&t=148s)

Ime Udoka: "For the most part, that's what he's done here. He started some games obviously with the injuries in and out, but his energy and the way he kind of reeks havoc off the bench is great for us. It's a change of pace. When he comes in, you're going out with our man and bringing him and Marcus, so the physicality and defensive intensity is still there. So yeah, he's shooting the ball well, knowing when to slash, mix it up, get out in transition and doing what he does usually on defense. So I love the boost he gives us."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4&t=148s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4&t=148s)

**5. Ime Udoka — "being more aggressive, trying to get downhill, touch the paint and really attack the basket" — Ime Udoka on Amen Thompson's increased aggressiveness** [03:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4&t=192s)

Ime Udoka: "Being more aggressive, trying to get downhill, touch the paint and really attack the basket. He's missed some early on in the season that, you know, we said we love those looks and I think just the concentrated effort to get to his spots and be more aggressive as far as finishing. And so, not really worried about that. It's some nights like I said, it's different guys and when he's aggressive we're that much better."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4&t=192s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4&t=192s)

**6. Ime Udoka — "we still don't want to just rely on those two, we got other guys that can score" — Ime Udoka on late-game offensive execution and team depth** [04:19](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4&t=259s)

Ime Udoka: "Yeah, I mean a lot's to do with the matchup and what's going well. We always feel like Alperen and Kevin have an advantage most nights and depending on how the defense plays it, other guys will kind of be recipients of that. But yes, you know, I think we still don't want to just rely on those two, we got other guys that can score. Obviously Fred is going to shoot the ball well, Amen is going to get downhill, Jabari will mix it all in and when the ball is moving and we're taking care of it and it's popping around the gym like tonight, 35 assists on 46 buckets, it's kind of equal opportunity and harder to guard."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4&t=259s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vmYnUII1ti4&t=259s)

## Philadelphia 76ers Postgame Media Availability 🎙️ | Coach Nurse | 11.23.26 — *Philadelphia 76ers*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xoH7GrTUPtE](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xoH7GrTUPtE)

_Speakers identified: Nick Nurse_

**1. Nick Nurse — "It's hard to lose by 10 on that particular stat. 10 makes, that's 30 points." — Coach Nurse on the 76ers' defensive struggles and three-point shooting** [00:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xoH7GrTUPtE&t=0s)

Nick Nurse: "First of all, they outplayed us. Their energy and hustle in general was excellent throughout the game. But the big thing is the three-point shooting. It's hard to lose by 10 on that particular stat. 10 makes, that's 30 points, that's hard to overcome unless you're going to beat them at the free-throw line extensively or win the turnover battle extensively. Possession battles were pretty even, and then they make 10 more threes than you. It's too bad, because I think we went 0-for-4 with **Jabari Smith Jr.**, Dean, and **Kentavious Caldwell-Pope**, and other than one of **Kentavious Caldwell-Pope**'s, they were all great shots. Maybe one of **Kentavious Caldwell-Pope**'s he had to arc it a little higher over Clingan coming out at him, but other than that, I think they were really good shots."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xoH7GrTUPtE&t=0s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xoH7GrTUPtE&t=0s)

<small>[Clip it](presserclips://clip?v=xoH7GrTUPtE&t=0&q=1) · [Send to Slack](presserclips://clip?v=xoH7GrTUPtE&t=0&q=1&slack=1)</small>

**2. Nick Nurse — "It was a huge turning point because he was really cooking." — Impact of Jalen Brunson's foul trouble on the game's momentum** [01:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xoH7GrTUPtE&t=90s)

Nick Nurse: "It was a huge turning point because he was really cooking. And even though they were shooting very well, we were able to at least match them at the offensive end, and had a bunch of calls in a row on him that really changed the complexion of the game."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xoH7GrTUPtE&t=90s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xoH7GrTUPtE&t=90s)

**3. Nick Nurse — "Your rim protection and inability to do that with some size is always a focal point." — Defensive rim protection and committee rebounding issues** [02:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xoH7GrTUPtE&t=143s)

Nick Nurse: "Your rim protection and inability to do that with some size is always a focal point of being really good defensively. Then you have to do it a little bit more by committee. I thought for the most part, I'm looking at possessions where I liked our help into the paint, they were doing a good job of zinging it out. I didn't particularly like our back out to get to them. There was a couple times they missed those and still got rebounds. We were compacted in the lane with everybody since we didn't get back out, and then giving up an offensive rebound as well. That's just playing through the possession, a little bit of energy and all that kind of stuff. I know it's a battle in there when you got a big guy in there to try to use a couple bodies on it, but we just didn't do enough pieces well."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xoH7GrTUPtE&t=143s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xoH7GrTUPtE&t=143s)

**4. Nick Nurse — "I thought Scoot got downhill on us a few times." — Defensive breakdowns and need for better help rotations** [04:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xoH7GrTUPtE&t=272s)

Nick Nurse: "Not good tonight. I think way too, I mean, I think Avdija got where he wanted to go too much, Dame did too, but they weren't the only ones. I thought Scoot got downhill on us a few times. There's two parts to it. The guy guarding them has got to be able to round out those drives a little bit more, and then if there is some help coming, the help's got to be there earlier and be better."

## How Warriors’ Al Horford would have fared in NCAA’s NIL era #DubsTalk #NBCSportsBayArea #basketball — *Golden State Warriors*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yCA8UofB80U](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yCA8UofB80U)

_Speakers identified: Unidentified speaker, Al Horford_

**1. "I hope that they're getting some sort of guidance" — Al Horford on the challenges of NIL for young college athletes** [00:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yCA8UofB80U&t=0s)

**Unidentified reporter:** "NIL, what are your thoughts about NIL and just how it's affected college and everything, just these kids? And also what would have been like back then maybe for you too with a little NIL?"

**Al Horford:** "I worry a little bit about the kids. I do want them to get paid and rewarded, but I hope that they're getting some sort of guidance because I've talked to some of the younger guys who are coming up and I'm asking questions and things like that. It's great that they're getting rewarded, they rightfully should, but then it's like we have to find a way to help them to put them in a better position so they can be successful with the money that they're making."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yCA8UofB80U&t=0s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yCA8UofB80U&t=0s)

**2. Al Horford — "I don't think I had the maturity level like that to handle that" — Al Horford reflects on his own maturity during his college career** [00:39](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yCA8UofB80U&t=39s)

Al Horford: "If I had NIL back then, I don't know if it would have been good for me. When you're 18, 19, all of a sudden you get all this money, it's so tempting to go buy a nice fancy car. For me, it was actually a good thing because I was driven to lock in and just get to the league, but I don't think I had the maturity level like that to handle that. So I would say that I'm okay with it, but I like NIL, but I just think that we need to find a way to help them so they can be better with their money."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yCA8UofB80U&t=39s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yCA8UofB80U&t=39s)

## Tyler Herro Postgame Media Availability | 11.23.26 — *Milwaukee Bucks*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yepXbE4K5gA](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yepXbE4K5gA)

_Speakers identified: Tyler Herro_

**1. Tyler Herro — "Anytime you can win in this league it feels good." — Tyler Herro on the difficulty of winning in the NBA** [00:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yepXbE4K5gA&t=16s)

Tyler Herro: "It feels good. This is a hard league. Anytime you can win in this league it feels good. Even the good teams, I feel like, not saying we are bad, but the teams that do win a lot of games, each game is meaningful and it is not easy to get wins in this league. The team we played over there is really good and they are still clawing and aching for wins. It is tough and it feels good to get out of there with a win. I feel like we can build on this, take this energy into Wednesday, enjoy tonight and try to get Wednesday."
